Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a lagrangian algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 444. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 574/1.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 444 + O(1/n^574). Summing over all modes and applying the lagrangian convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
